Session 34 result (the alpha-test depth prepass test; the user at the machine at the start (HIDIdleTime 9 s): the online test "Later, not now", "Run them now" for the runs that take the cursor; then away; no code change, only the docs; commits on `metal`, not pushed: Phase 5 isn't done):
- **Measured first** (§2 "The alpha-tested draws' GPU time, the depth prepass and the draw order"): the Space at 1920×1080 in the decoupled mode, at 960 and uncapped, on mohdm5, obj_team1, obj_team2, mohdm4 and m2l1. The GPU limits uncapped on mohdm5 and the obj maps (busy 95–99%), and on mohdm5 already makes the CPU wait now and then at 960 (GPU 0.74–0.88 ms a frame). The alpha-tested draws (static tree and bush models, treeline and hedgerow cards) are up to 75% of the GPU's time on those views (mohdm5 ~75%).
- **The prepass prototype: dropped.** Per alpha-tested batch, depth only then shaded with depth EQUAL: mohdm5 +11–15% GPU time at 960 and −4 to −11% frames/s uncapped, obj_team2 worse too, only obj_team1 uncapped gains (+0–11%). MOHAA's foliage is one texture times the vertex color, so the depth pass costs what the shading did.
- **Found instead: draw the alpha-tested surfaces after the opaque ones.** A stable partition of the opaque range after the sort: mohdm5 −8 to −26% GPU time at 960, uncapped up to +20% frames/s; neutral (±4% at 960, ±2% uncapped) on obj_team1, mohdm4 and m2l1; obj_team2 uncapped +0 to +7%, at 960 one view +14% where the GPU is 35% busy. The image differs only in tie pixels (≤ 90 of 921,600, max 17). Front to back within a shader adds −1.5 to −4% on mohdm5. At 480 / 250 the GPU's clock governor blurs it (one run: mixed at 480, −6 to −31% at 250). Implementing it is session 35's slice.
- **Every timing segment's frames on a performance core** (≥ 99.8%) but one (84.4%, obj_team2 uncapped). No bench: no code changed (`s34x` is HEAD plus the scratch instrumentation).
- **Tools** (scratch, `build.noindex/s34/`): `inst34.py` (S34TMP, applied to a source copy: the per-frame log `r_s34log` / `r_s34view` / `r_s34dump`, `r_s34skipAT`, `r_s34prepass`, the one-frame draw list `r_s34list 1` (shader, indices, alpha test, depth write and test, blend, textures), `r_s34ftb` 1 front to back / 2 both / 3 alpha-tested after opaque), `build.sh` (the flavor `s34x` = HEAD + the work tree's diff + `inst34.py`), `mkcfg.py` + `run34.sh <name> <map> <view>...` (env `TOGGLE`, `TOGGLEON`, `RATES`, `ARGS`), `an34.py` (the table), `shotab.sh` (the off/on image comparison), `docs34.py`; logs in `build.noindex/s34/logs`, frame logs in `home.noindex/s34/`.

Session 35 result (the alpha-tested surfaces after the opaque ones; the user away, who asked for the session to run without them (HIDIdleTime 34 s at the start, two single touches by 13:03, then idle; unlocked throughout): no online test, no synthetic mouse; commits on `metal`, not pushed: Phase 5 isn't done):
- **Done** (§3.2 "Added in session 35"): `alphaTestLast` in `shader_t`, set by `FinishShader` for an `SS_OPAQUE` shader with an alpha-tested, depth-writing stage; `SortNewShader` sorts by (`sort`, `alphaTestLast`), so those shaders' surfaces come after the other opaque ones in every frame's sort, with no per-frame work; `shader->sort` stays `SS_OPAQUE` (the dlight tests and the rest read it; every reader of the order checked).
- **GPU time** (§2 "The alpha-tested surfaces after the opaque ones, in the shaders' order"; the Space at 1920×1080, the decoupled mode, A B A B per view and rate, every segment's frames on a performance core at ≥ 98.7%): **mohdm5 −8 to −27% at 960** (commit → GPU done p50 0.92 / 0.85 / 0.88 / 1.01 → 0.81 / 0.68 / 0.67 / 0.95 ms), **+10 to +26% frames/s uncapped**, 480 −0 to −13%, 250 −1 to −23%; obj_team2 and mohdm4 −0 to −13%; obj_team1 and m2l1 ±1–4% (one cell slower: obj_team1 yaw 0 uncapped, +0.6% GPU). The clock governor made session 34's one slower view (obj_team2 yaw 90 at 960): repeated, −13%.
- **No CPU cost** (the radix sorts' time per frame equal with either order; the CPU-bound uncapped views as fast or faster). **Bench** (ABBA, `metalref` = HEAD before the change, 3 runs each): metalref 0.217 / 0.222 / 0.222 and 0.224 / 0.215 / 0.226, metal 0.216 / 0.219 / 0.220 and 0.225 / 0.224 / 0.223 ms work: means 0.2210 → 0.2212, equal; pooled p50/p95/p99/p99.9 metalref 210/371/490/725 and 213/371/484/670, metal 208/367/489/656 and 214/381/491/706 µs.
- **The image:** tie pixels only (`regress base-s29`: 1–46 pixels of 921,600 in seven of the 30 views, max 2–86; with `s35x`'s `r_s35order 0` every view that doesn't vary from run to run matches base-s29 exactly). A deliberate difference (§3.1 #20); **the fixed views' baseline is `base-s35`** (`port/shot.sh regress base-s35`). `port/glcheck.sh`: clean.
- **Not taken:** the static models front to back within a shader on top (mohdm5 −0.3 to −3.6% GPU at 960 for 3.3–4.1 µs of CPU a frame; mohdm4 nothing for 1.8–3.8 µs).
- **Found:** `R_SortDrawSurfs`' radix sorts take 24–41 µs a frame on mohdm5 and 14–30 on mohdm4 at 1920×1080 (1–10 µs on the obj maps and m2l1): a CPU candidate for session 36 (§6).
- **Tools** (scratch, `build.noindex/s35/`): `inst35.py` (S35TMP, after `s34/inst34.py`: `r_s35order` 0 = GL1's order, re-sorting `tr.sortedShaders` at the frame's start and in `SortNewShader`; "S35 sort" lines = the radix sorts' and `r_s34ftb`'s front-to-back CPU µs a frame per `r_s34view` segment), `build.sh` (the flavor `s35x` = HEAD + the work tree's diff + `inst34.py` + `inst35.py`), `mkcfg35.py` + `run35.sh <name> <map> <view>...` (A B A B per view and rate; env `RATES`, `REPS`, `TOGGLE`, `TOGGLEON`, `SETTLE`, `HOLD`, `ARGS`), `an35.py` (per rep and the means; commit → done), `table35.py` (the §2 table), `batch35.sh` (runs in a row, stopping if someone used the machine in the last 60 s); logs in `build.noindex/s35/logs`, frame logs in `home.noindex/s35/`.

Session 36 result (the draw surfaces' sort; the user at the machine at the start (HIDIdleTime 1.7–20 s), who said "Later, not now" to the online test and OK'd the runs that take the cursor; unlocked throughout; no synthetic mouse; commits on `metal`, not pushed: Phase 5 isn't done):
- **Done** (§3.2 "Added in session 36"): `R_RadixSort` sorts the runs of equal keys instead of the surfaces (one copy pass, an LSD radix sort of `key << 32 | run` over the key bits that vary, the surfaces copied back run by run); the same order byte for byte.
- **Measured first** (§2 "The draw surfaces' sort by runs of equal keys"): one sort a frame on session 35's views; mohdm5 4,600–4,900 surfaces in 370–606 runs (~300 distinct keys), mohdm4 1,900–3,500 in 830–1,750, the obj maps and m2l1 57–1,260 (1.2–2.3 a run); the dlight byte constant there (three 8-bit passes), all four bytes varying on the bench (646 surfaces, 10 sprites a frame). The old sort's ~8.5 ns a surface is the counters' chain through memory where neighbours share a digit; (key, index) pairs per surface keep it (slower still offline).
- **CPU per frame at 960** (the Space at 1920×1080, the decoupled mode, A B A B per view, ≥ 99.9% of the frames on a performance core): the sort on mohdm5 38.6–40.2 → 6.4–8.2 µs and the frame's work −31 to −43 µs (456–578 → 425–535, **−7%**); mohdm4 15.4–28.3 → 8.1–14.7 µs (work −6 to −15); obj_team2 2.6–6.0 → 1.8–4.6; obj_team1 0.7–2.7 → 0.7–2.5 (yaw 0 +0.4 µs); m2l1 9.7 → 7.1 and 2.55 → 2.65.
- **Bench** (ABBA, `metalref` = HEAD before the change, 3 runs each): metalref 0.220 / 0.225 / 0.224 and 0.220 / 0.229 / 0.223, metal 0.220 / 0.220 / 0.221 and 0.225 / 0.221 / 0.224 ms work: means 0.2235 → 0.2218 (equal, inside the runs' spread); pooled p50/p95/p99/p99.9 metalref 211/374/496/1038 and 210/387/537/1183, metal 210/374/498/774 and 212/380/513/705 µs. The bench's sort alone: 4.85 → 4.45 µs a frame.
- **The image:** bit-exact. `r_s36check 1`: 0 of 34,500 sorts differ from the old order. `port/shot.sh regress base-s35`: identical except the captures that vary from run to run, the same ones `metalref` differs in. `base-s35` stays the baseline. `port/glcheck.sh`: clean.
- **Not taken:** (key, index) pairs per surface, the counting pass folded into `R_AddDrawSurf`, the counts on the stack, a fallback for inputs without runs (§2).
- **Tools** (scratch, `build.noindex/s36/`): `inst36.py` (S36TMP: b7f1729e's `R_RadixSort` kept as `S36_RadixSortOld`, `r_s36sort` 0 = old, 1 = the tree's, 2 = the tree's with the counts on the stack; `r_s36check 1` sorts a copy with the old one and counts the differing sorts (printed at the quit); `r_s36log 1` per-frame records dumped to `r_s36dump` at the renderer's shutdown: view tag, the sorts' ns, surfaces, sprites, calls, the old sort's varying bytes, core, the frame's work µs, the largest call; `r_s36keys <tag>` dumps the next frame's sort inputs), `build.sh` (the flavor `s36x` = HEAD + the work tree's diff + `inst36.py`; after this session's commit the tree's sort is the new one and `S36_RadixSortOld` is still b7f1729e's), `mkcfg36.py` + `run36.sh <name> <map> <view>...` (A B A B per view at 960, then a check second; env `RATES`, `REPS`, `TOGGLE`, `TOGGLEA` / `TOGGLEB`, `CHECK`, `SETTLE`, `HOLD`, `ARGS`), `batch36.sh` (the five maps), `an36.py` (per segment and per view), `keys36.py` (runs and distinct keys of a dump), `sortbench.c` (the variants offline on the dumps, shuffled and random inputs; exits if any differs from the old order); logs in `build.noindex/s36/logs`, frame logs and dumps in `home.noindex/s36/`.

Session 37 result (the off-screen skeletal models, JayRewd's b26d8a67 idea re-implemented; the user away at the start (HIDIdleTime 2 → 570 s), back at 15:51, who said "Later, not now" to the online test and OK'd the runs that take the cursor; unlocked throughout; no synthetic mouse; commits on `metal`, not pushed: Phase 5 isn't done):
- **Done** (§3.2 "Added in session 37"): `R_AddSkelSurfaces` returns before the frame, the bones, the morphs and the surfaces for a model outside a side plane of the view (its animation radius × 1.1 + 16); past the far plane alone, or with the skeletor's no-animation radius, it is drawn as before.
- **Measured first** (§2 "The off-screen skeletal models"): on the bench 9.1 of 17.5 skeletal models a frame culled, adding 35 of its 69 skeletal surfaces; live 8-bot maps 7.5–19.3 of 11.7–33.7, single-player soldiers 6–28 of 34–46. The animation's radius isn't strictly conservative (vertexes up to 6.5 units, 7.6%, past it on soldiers; 3.5 past a muzzle flash's 2.6), hence the margin; with it, 3.3 million model checks over 140,000 frames (the bench, bots, 17 single-player maps with AI, vehicles, boats) found no culled model with a vertex in view.
- **CPU per frame at 960** (the Space at 1920×1080, the decoupled mode, paired alternating frames, ≥ 99.9% on a performance core): the frame's work mohdm6 with 8 bots 175.0 → 154.4 µs (−11.8%), mohdm4 431.6 → 390.3 (−9.6%), obj_team2 275.1 → 249.2 (−9.4%), m1l1's start −11.5 to −15.5% (256–421 → 226–366), m3l1a's −8.1 to −11.0%; `R_AddSkelSurfaces` −40 to −45% there. A culled model was ~0.6 µs of front end and ~2–3 µs of the frame's work in all (the backend's surface building and skin jobs).
- **Bench** (ABBA, `metalref` = HEAD before the change, 3 runs each): metalref 0.224 / 0.223 / 0.225 and 0.224 / 0.219 / 0.219, metal 0.208 / 0.201 / 0.192 and 0.202 / 0.195 / 0.196 ms work: means **0.2223 → 0.1990 (−10.5%)**; pooled p50/p95/p99/p99.9 metalref 212/389/515/909 and 207/384/511/885, metal 188/358/480/1089 and 186/351/475/668 µs. In `s37x`, the same frames alternating: 224.8 → 200.8 µs.
- **The image:** bit-exact. `port/shot.sh regress base-s35`: identical except the 30 captures that vary from run to run, the same 30 `metalref` differs in. `base-s35` stays the baseline. `port/glcheck.sh`: clean.
- **Not taken:** skipping past the far plane alone (only m3l1a yaw 90 had any, 4 a frame, and whether a fogged model shows depends on the map); a case for personal models (none on these views); JayRewd's `r_verifySkelCull` as a shipped cvar (the scratch check covers it; `s37x` keeps it).
- **Tools** (scratch, `build.noindex/s37/`): `inst37.py` (S37TMP: the tree's skip through `S37_Skip`, which classifies each call (drawn, out by a side plane, out by the far plane alone, personal, out by the radius alone but kept by the margin); `r_s37skip <mask>` 0 = GL1's path, 1 = the tree's (2 = also the far plane, 4 = also personal models); `r_s37margin` / `r_s37factor` (16 / 1.1); `r_s37alt 1` alternates mask and 0 frame by frame; `r_s37check 1` = GL1's path plus every model's vertexes skinned on the CPU and tested against the view (misses, overshoot; per-tiki maxima at the quit); `r_s37log 1` per-frame records dumped to `r_s37dump`: classes, the calls' ns, surfaces, bones, the check, the scene-reset counter, core, the frame's work), `build.sh` (`s37x` = HEAD + the work tree's diff + `inst37.py`), `mkcfg37.py` + `run37.sh <name> <map> <view>...` (views `tele`/`face`, `follow` = spectate a bot, `stay`; A B A B, then alternating frames, then a check; env `BOTS`, `RATES`, `REPS`, `ALT`, `CHECK`, `PRE`, `TOGGLEA` / `TOGGLEB`), `batch37.sh` (the bot maps and m1l1 / m3l1a; env `P` = a prefix), `bench37.sh` (the timedemo 0 1 1 0 and alternating), `sweep37.sh` (the check over 17 single-player maps), `an37.py` (per segment); logs in `build.noindex/s37/logs`, frame logs in `home.noindex/s37/`.

Plan (session 1):
- Latency work from §4b (frames-in-flight tuning, GCMouse raw input, the client timing redesign: precise pacing by default + the optional decoupled command/render mode + the percentile cap suggestion + low-latency vsync, activity/QoS/Game Mode, audio latency), measured with the latency probe.
- Measure first (bench + `trace` + `sample`/Time Profiler), one change at a time, each recorded here with before/after numbers. Candidates:
  - Static world geometry in GPU-resident buffers (no per-frame re-tessellation/copy).
  - Batching by shader+lightmap; sort keys to minimize PSO/state changes; fewer render passes.
  - GPU skinning (compute) for TIKI; GPU tcMod/tcGen/rgbGen/deforms for the common cases.
  - Faster `RB_Light_Real` sphere lighting.
  - BC texture compression for memory; async texture loading at map load.
  - Metal 4 (argument tables, residency sets, `MTL4CommandQueue`) where measured.
  - Frame pacing (present timing / CAMetalDisplayLink).
- Engine frame floor: `Com_Frame` pads frames to 1 ms with an integer-ms clock, which caps fps near 1000. Making it µs-precise changes how `com_maxfps` behaves online (frame pacing feeds usercmd timing and movement feel), so **ask the user before touching it**.
- **Done when:** the bench work time is ≤ 0.25 ms/frame or the remaining cost is documented as engine-side, p99 frame time is lower than GL1, input→present latency is measured lower than the Phase 2 numbers (and as low as the display mode allows), and the fixed views (`port/shot.sh pair`) show no visual change (since §3.1 #20: no change that isn't deliberate and recorded).
  - *Status after session 14:* work time **met** (0.234 ms, the rest documented above); fixed views **met** (every change, `regress base-s9`); **p99 vs GL1 met** (session 14: work p99 739 µs vs GL1 2318, p99.9 1536 vs 3490, pooled over 8 runs each, unlocked; after 5c ~500 µs, 4.7× below GL1); **latency open** (after Phase 6, on the native input layer, §3.1 #8; `port/latency.sh` takes the mouse: with the user). *Session 26:* exclusive fullscreen and the borderless window no longer lose frames while another application draws on the other display (5d, late presents); the probe check against the Phase 2 numbers with the synthetic mouse still needs the user. *Session 28:* the GPU is woken when a frame starts (5f): commit → on screen ~0.35 ms shorter at 250 fps (Space 2.36 → 2.00 ms p50).

### Phase 6: Native AppKit/GameController (replace SDL2). **Status: done (6a, the survey and design, done in session 18, 2026-10-18; 6b, the application and the window, done in session 19, 2026-10-18; 6c, the keyboard and text, done in session 20, 2026-10-18; 6d, the mouse, done in session 21, 2026-10-18; 6e, the rest, done in session 22, 2026-10-18; 6f's synthetic half, the latency sitting, done in session 23, 2026-10-18: native matches SDL; 6g, SDL out with a build of the last SDL commit kept (`sdlref`), done in session 24, 2026-10-18, §3.1 #17; 6f's user half against `sdlref`, with the Cmd+Tab fix for exclusive fullscreen with a mode switch, done in session 25, 2026-10-18, §3.1 #18; approved by the user in the reorder, §3.1 #8).**
- First, the baseline: SDL's input latency with the probe (`port/latency.sh`, takes the mouse, so with the user), in the display modes of §2.
- Then native `NSWindow` + `CAMetalLayer` view, keyboard (NSEvent → Q3 keys, text input), raw mouse (GCMouse deltas, cursor capture; moved here from the Phase 5 latency list), ~~gamepad (GCController)~~ (dropped by the user, §3.1 #12), clipboard, window modes, Retina, ~~the `openmohaa://` URL handler~~ (removed instead, the user, §3.1 #13), the color cursor.
- The native input should give precise input timestamps (event/GCMouse timestamps on the host clock) for the probe.
- **Done when:** no SDL in `otool -L` (done, session 24), input matches or beats SDL on the probe (the synthetic mouse: done, session 23) and in the user's feel test at the end (no mouse acceleration), menus/console/text input work. *Met in session 25: GCMouse equal to the movement events on one clock, the feel test "The same", "Same speed, no accel", menus, console and typing fine ("Session 25 result").*
- **Session 18 result: 6a, survey and design** (2026-10-18; read and plan, no code change). The SDL baseline moves: **the user agreed (2026-10-18, asked in session 18) to measure it later, in one sitting with the native layer's first numbers**, from an SDL flavor kept buildable until the last slice ("How it's built" below).
  - **What SDL does for the game today.** The client binary imports 80 SDL functions (`nm -u`), from these files:
    - **`code/sdl/sdl_mtlimp.m`** (renderer side, `MTLimp_*`):
      - `SDL_Init(VIDEO)`, which also creates the application: SDL's `NSApplication` subclass, the menu bar, its app delegate, `finishLaunching`, and three registered user defaults (`ApplePressAndHoldEnabled` NO, `AppleMomentumScrollSupported` NO, `ApplePersistenceIgnoreState` YES);
      - the window (`SDL_CreateWindow`: hidden, Metal, high-DPI; resizable with `r_allowResize`; borderless with `r_noborder` in a window), placed by `r_displayIndex`/`r_centerWindow`, the old position kept across `vid_restart`;
      - exclusive fullscreen (`SDL_WINDOW_FULLSCREEN`: a borderless window at the shielding level, `SDL_SetWindowDisplayMode` → `CGDisplaySetDisplayMode`, the desktop mode restored at exit) and borderless fullscreen (`SDL_WINDOW_FULLSCREEN_DESKTOP`: a native fullscreen Space);
      - `SDL_Metal_CreateView` and its `CAMetalLayer`, `SDL_Metal_GetDrawableSize` (pixels → `glConfig.vidWidth/vidHeight`), `hasShadow = NO` after `SDL_ShowWindow` in fullscreen;
      - `r_availableModes` (the display's modes, one per size, sorted by aspect, then area), `MTLimp_Minimize`;
      - `vid_restart` = `SDL_QuitSubSystem(VIDEO)` and `SDL_Init(VIDEO)` again; `ri.IN_Init(SDL_window)`;
      - dead: the window icon (`USE_ICON` is never defined, so `SDL_SetWindowIcon(NULL)`; the bundle's `openmohaa.icns` is the icon, and `sdl_icon.h` is unused) and `r_sdlDriver` (ROM, "cocoa", read by nothing).
    - **`code/sdl/sdl_input.c`** (client side, `IN_*`):
      - `IN_Frame`, once per `Com_Frame`, after the frame limiter and before `Com_EventLoop` (`common.c`): the joystick, then mouse capture on or off, then `IN_ProcessEvents`, the `SDL_PollEvent` loop (`SDL_PumpEvents` = Cocoa's `nextEventMatchingMask:untilDate:distantPast` loop with `sendEvent:`, then SDL's own queue), then `in_eventTime`, then the delayed `vid_restart` after a resize. The capture is off with the console or UI up in a window, while loading in a window, and without input focus.
      - Keyboard (`IN_TranslateSDLToQ3Key`): the number row by scancode; a `sym` in [space, delete) is its ASCII value (the layout's unshifted character); the grave scancode is `` ` `` (OPM, for the console); a table of named keys; anything else `K_WORLD_0` + scancode (≤ 95); Cmd is `K_COMMAND`; `cl_consoleKeys` ("~ \` 0x7e 0x60") → `K_CONSOLE`. Repeats are dropped unless a key catcher is up. `SE_CHAR` also for backspace (^H), enter/tab/escape (OPM) and Ctrl+letter. `in_keyboardDebug` prints each key.
      - Text: `SDL_StartTextInput` is always on; `SDL_TEXTINPUT` UTF-8 → UTF-32 `SE_CHAR`, a console character becomes a `K_CONSOLE` press and release, and the character right after a console key is dropped.
      - Mouse: relative mode plus window grab; `in_mouse` (0 = off), `in_nograb` (windowed only; fullscreen is refused with it). `SDL_MOUSEMOTION` → `SE_MOUSE` (xrel, yrel); buttons left/middle/right/X1/X2 → `K_MOUSE1`–5, more → `K_AUX`; the wheel → `K_MWHEELUP`/`DOWN` press and release. Releasing the capture warps to the center; `IN_GobbleMotionEvents` drops pending motion when the capture changes.
      - Window events: resized → `r_customwidth/height` and `vid_restart` after 1 s; minimized/restored → `com_minimized`; focus → `com_unfocused`; `SDL_QUIT` (close button, ⌘Q through SDL's app delegate) → `quit Closed window`.
      - Gamepad and joystick: `SDL_Init(JOYSTICK)` always, even with `in_joystick 0` (the default), so SDL's joystick subsystem and its HID manager run anyway; the game controller subsystem only with `in_joystick 1`. `in_availableJoysticks`; a gamepad → `K_PAD0_*` buttons and axes (`joy_threshold` dead zone; `in_joystickUseAnalog` → `SE_JOYSTICK_AXIS` through the `+forward`… binds); a plain joystick → `K_JOY1`–32 buttons, hats, balls (`SE_MOUSE`) and axes; hot plug re-inits.
      - `SDL_DROPFILE` with an `openmohaa:` URL → `Sys_ParseProtocolUri` → `connect`: **compiled out.** `PROTOCOL_HANDLER` is never defined (upstream's CMake rewrite, e556f58c, kept `PROTOCOL_HANDLER_SCHEME` for the Info.plist but not the C define), so the app registers `openmohaa://` and then ignores the URL; `--uri` too.
    - **`code/sdl/sdl_mouse.c`:**
      - `IN_GetMousePosition` (`SDL_GetMouseState`: the absolute position in window points, which `CL_UpdateMouse` compares with pixel sizes);
      - `IN_SetCursorFromImage` (an SDL color cursor from the UI's RGBA image, hotspot 0,0; the image is never freed, an upstream leak), `IN_FreeCursor`;
      - `IN_IsCursorActive` (= relative mode on).
    - **`code/sys/sys_main.c`:** the SDL include; `SDL_WasInit(VIDEO)` in `Sys_In_Restart_f`; `SDL_Quit` in `Sys_Exit`; `Sys_GetProcessorFeatures` from `SDL_HasRDTSC/3DNow/MMX/SSE/SSE2/AltiVec` (all false on arm64, so it returns 0); the SDL version check at the start of `main`.
    - **`code/sys/new/sys_main_new.c`:** `Sys_GetWholeClipboard`/`Sys_SetClipboard` (`SDL_Get/SetClipboardText`; `sys_main.c`'s `Sys_GetClipboardData` is a NULL stub).
    - **`code/sys/sys_loadlib.h`:** `Sys_LoadLibrary` = `SDL_LoadObject` = **`dlopen(path, RTLD_NOW | RTLD_LOCAL)`** (read from SDL's binary: `mov w1, #0x6`), error "Failed loading %s: %s" with `dlerror()`; `SDL_LoadFunction` = `dlsym`, retried with a leading underscore. The `DEDICATED` branch already calls `dlopen(f, RTLD_NOW)` (global on macOS).
    - **`code/sys/sys_local.h`:** `SDL_version.h` and `MINSDL_*`.
    - **Build:** `cmake/libraries/sdl.cmake`, `client.cmake` (`sdl_input.c`, `sdl_mouse.c`), `renderer_metal.cmake` (`sdl_mtlimp.m`), `macos.cmake` (the arm64 thinning of `CLIENT_DEPLOY_LIBRARIES`), `code/thirdparty/SDL2-2.32.8/include`, `thirdparty/libs/macos/libSDL2-2.0.0.dylib` and `libSDL2main.a` (linked but unused on macOS; ld warns about it twice), glcheck's SDL exception.
    - **Already native, staying:** `sys_osx.m`'s `Sys_Dialog` (NSAlert; it works without SDL) and the probe's NSEvent monitor (it goes once the pump records event times itself); `sys_unix.c`'s `Sys_ErrorDialog` calls `Sys_Dialog`.
  - **Findings that shape the design** (from SDL's arm64 binary with `otool -tV`, the SDK headers and session 14's profile):
    1. **The mouse deltas are the system cursor's accelerated deltas, cut to integers.** `Cocoa_HandleMouseEvent` passes `(int)[event deltaX]` (`fcvtzs`, toward zero) to `SDL_SendMouseMotion`. So macOS pointer acceleration applies (this Mac has the system default: acceleration on, `com.apple.mouse.linear`/`scaling` unset), and every fractional part is dropped per event: slow, fine aim below one point per event is lost (±0.9 → 0). GCMouse's `mouseMovedHandler` gives the "raw mouse delta … not affected by mouse sensitivity settings" (SDK header).
    2. **The bundled SDL2 is an unoptimized build** (every function spills its arguments to the stack: `-O0` code), and it brings 18 frameworks into the process (Carbon, IOKit, ForceFeedback, CoreHaptics, AVFoundation, CoreAudio, AudioToolbox, GameController, …).
    3. **Its per-frame cost is small:** in session 14's looping-timedemo profile, 158 of 16618 main-thread samples are under `IN_Frame` (~1%: ~6–10 µs per frame), mostly the run loop's mach port calls, which a native pump makes too. The native layer won't change throughput; its value is the input path itself (raw deltas, no acceleration, no truncation, event times) and leanness.
    4. **SDL minimizes an exclusive-fullscreen window on focus loss** (`SDL_VIDEO_MINIMIZE_ON_FOCUS_LOSS` unset = "auto": exclusive fullscreen, not a Space) and restores the desktop mode; borderless fullscreen (a Space) is left alone.
    5. **`port/latency.sh`'s synthetic mouse can't reach GCMouse.** `mousemove.c` posts CGEvents at the HID event tap, where events enter the WindowServer; GCMouse reads the HID devices below it. So the probe measures the NSEvent path (and SDL) as before, but GCMouse only with a real mouse: the user moves theirs during the probe run. (Expected from the APIs; 6d checks it once.)
    6. **`openmohaa://` is registered but inert** (above). *Removed in session 22 (§3.1 #13).*
    7. **HiDPI input bugs today** (2× displays only; the main display is 1×): the resize check compares points with pixels; the windowed UI's absolute cursor is in points against a pixel-sized UI; the warp to the center passes pixels as points; relative deltas are points while the fullscreen UI cursor moves in pixels (half speed at 2×).
  - **The native design.** New files in `code/macos/` (upstream merges then conflict only on the deleted SDL files, as with 7a): `macos_app.m` (the application, the pump, clipboard, library loading), `macos_window.m` (renderer side: the same `MTLimp_*` functions as `sdl_mtlimp.m`), `macos_input.m` (client side: the same `IN_*` functions as `sdl_input.c` and `sdl_mouse.c`), and a small `macos_local.h` between them. About 1.5k lines of Objective-C (ARC).
    - **The run loop:**
      - `NSApplication` without `[NSApp run]`, created once: regular activation policy, a minimal menu bar (the app menu with Hide, Hide Others, Show All, Quit ⌘Q), `finishLaunching`, activate. The delegate's `applicationShouldTerminate:` queues `quit` and returns `NSTerminateCancel` (the engine exits through `Sys_Quit`). The URL handler (Apple event `kInternetEventClass`/`kAEGetURL`) is installed before `finishLaunching`, under `PROTOCOL_HANDLER`.
      - The three user defaults SDL registers: without `ApplePressAndHoldEnabled` NO, holding a key opens the accent popup instead of repeating; without `ApplePersistenceIgnoreState` YES, macOS restores windows.
      - One pump per `Com_Frame` in `IN_Frame`, where SDL's is: `nextEventMatchingMask:NSEventMaskAny untilDate:nil inMode:NSDefaultRunLoopMode dequeue:YES` until empty, each event handled and passed to `sendEvent:` where AppKit needs it, in one `@autoreleasepool`. Never a blocking wait. The pump also drains the main dispatch queue (GameController's notifications), as SDL's does.
      - `vid_restart` destroys and recreates only the window; the application and the input devices stay.
    - **The window:**
      - `NSWindow` with a layer-hosting content view (`view.layer = metalLayer; view.wantsLayer = YES`) that accepts first responder and is the text input client (below). The `CAMetalLayer` settings are unchanged (opaque, the drawable count, `displaySyncEnabled`, `presentsWithTransaction` NO); `drawableSize` = content size × `backingScaleFactor`, `contentsScale` = `backingScaleFactor`.
      - A window: titled, closable, miniaturizable (resizable with `r_allowResize`), borderless with `r_noborder`; `r_displayIndex`, `r_centerWindow`, the old position kept across `vid_restart`.
      - Exclusive fullscreen (`r_fullscreen 1`): a borderless window at `CGShieldingWindowLevel()` over the screen, no shadow, dock and menu bar hidden (`presentationOptions`). `CGDisplaySetDisplayMode` only when `r_mode`'s size differs from the current mode (SDL switched even to the same mode and back at exit: one display reconfiguration fewer), restored at exit and on focus loss, where the window is minimized as SDL does (finding 4).
      - Borderless fullscreen (`r_fullscreen 1` + `r_noborder 1`): the native fullscreen Space (`NSWindowCollectionBehaviorFullScreenPrimary`, `toggleFullScreen:`), like `SDL_WINDOW_FULLSCREEN_DESKTOP`. The window is created at the desktop size, so the drawable is right before the Space's (asynchronous) animation. **Experiment in 6b:** a borderless window over the screen at the normal level with the dock and menu bar hidden by `presentationOptions` (session 6 tried this without hiding the menu bar: "display rejected"). If it goes direct to display, alt-tab and `vid_restart` skip the Space animation. Keep whichever goes direct (`display_trace.py`).
      - Retina: `glConfig.vidWidth/vidHeight` stay the drawable's pixels; every window coordinate the input uses is converted with `backingScaleFactor` (fixes finding 7); a backing-scale change (the window moved between the 1× and 2× displays) queues `vid_restart` like a resize.
      - `r_availableModes` from `CGDisplayCopyAllDisplayModes`, built like SDL's (same sizes, format and order): 6b compares the startup log's "Available modes" line with SDL's on this Mac.
      - Focus: `windowDidBecomeKey`/`ResignKey` → `com_unfocused`; miniaturize → `com_minimized`; the close button → `quit Closed window`; `MTLimp_Minimize` → miniaturize.
    - **Keyboard and text:**
      - Key events are handled in the pump (`keyDown`/`keyUp`/`flagsChanged`), as SDL does; the content view swallows `keyDown:` so nothing beeps, and ⌘ key equivalents still reach the menu through `sendEvent:`.
      - The `kVK_*` virtual key codes (physical positions) map to SDL's scancode numbering (USB HID usage IDs; a fixed 128-entry table written from the `kVK` and HID usage definitions), so `K_WORLD_0` + scancode and every other key resolve exactly as now, and saved binds keep working. Including SDL's ISO swap (`kVK_ISO_Section` and `kVK_ANSI_Grave` on ISO keyboards).
      - The layout character (`sym`): `UCKeyTranslate` with the current keyboard layout (`TISCopyCurrentKeyboardLayoutInputSource`, rebuilt when the input source changes), unshifted, no dead keys, as SDL builds its keymap. That needs Carbon (HIToolbox) linked again, which 7b dropped from our link; SDL links it today, so it isn't new to the process.
      - Modifiers from `flagsChanged` with the device-dependent left/right bits → `K_SHIFT`/`K_CTRL`/`K_ALT`/`K_COMMAND` down and up per side, like SDL's LSHIFT/RSHIFT; caps lock reported as SDL reports it (checked in 6c).
      - Repeat from `isARepeat`, dropped without a key catcher, as now. `in_keyboardDebug` prints the key code, scancode, character and Q3 key.
      - Text: each `keyDown` also goes through `interpretKeyEvents:` on the content view, a minimal `NSTextInputClient` (dead keys and input methods work); `insertText:` → `SE_CHAR` (UTF-32) with `sdl_input.c`'s console handling; `doCommandBySelector:` ignored (the key went in already); marked text stays out of the game (SDL's `SDL_TEXTEDITING` is ignored today too).
      - **Checked without taking the user's keyboard:** an in-process replay that posts NSEvents into the app's own queue (`[NSApp postEvent:atStart:]`; nothing leaves the process), driven by a cfg in a scratch build of both flavors, compares the `SE_KEY`/`SE_CHAR` streams of SDL and native.
    - **Mouse:**
      - **`in_mouseRaw`** (new, archived): 1 = GCMouse raw deltas; 0 = the NSEvent deltas cut toward zero per event exactly as SDL does (the like-for-like baseline, and the fallback). **Default 0 until the user's feel test**: raw deltas change aim feel and sensitivity (no acceleration; device counts instead of points), so the user picks the default then (planned: 1, the "no mouse acceleration" of Done when). *Picked by the user before 6f: 1 (§3.1 #14).*
      - GCMouse: watched through `GCMouseDidConnect`/`Disconnect`/`BecomeCurrent`; `handlerQueue` = a serial dispatch queue at `QOS_CLASS_USER_INTERACTIVE`; `mouseMovedHandler` adds the deltas (float sums, fractions carried over) and the times into an accumulator under an `os_unfair_lock`; `IN_Frame` takes it once per pump (one `SE_MOUSE`). Used only while captured and focused; the UI cursor uses NSEvents. 6d checks the y sign (GameController's y may point up) and `lastEventTimestamp`'s clock (undocumented: compared with `CLOCK_UPTIME_RAW` once; used as the event time if it is that clock, else the handler's own time).
      - Capture (= SDL's relative mode plus grab): warp the cursor to the window center in global points (`CGWarpMouseCursorPosition`, with `CGEventSourceSetLocalEventsSuppressionInterval` 0 so a warp doesn't freeze input for 0.25 s), `CGAssociateMouseAndMouseCursorPosition(false)`, `[NSCursor hide]`; release reverses it. Same rules as `IN_Frame` now, with `in_mouse` and `in_nograb`.
      - Buttons and wheel: NSEvent (`buttonNumber` → `K_MOUSE1`–5 and `K_AUX`, as SDL numbers them; the wheel's sign → the wheel keys; momentum off). 6d also logs GCMouse's button times next to the NSEvent times of the same clicks: if the GameController path is measurably earlier (≥ ~0.2 ms), buttons move to GCMouse while captured.
      - UI cursor: windowed menus read the absolute position (`mouseLocationOutsideOfEventStream` → view → pixels: fixes the 2× bug); captured menus (fullscreen) take NSEvent deltas × `backingScaleFactor` (the same feel on 1×).
      - The color cursor: an `NSCursor` from an `NSImage` of the RGBA pixels, hotspot 0,0 (and the image freed: the upstream leak).
      - The probe: event times come straight from the pump (`NSEvent.timestamp`) and from the GCMouse accumulator, so `Sys_MouseEventMonitor` goes; `CL_LatencyPump` stays at the pump start. Enabled for later (Phase 5 timing work, needs the user): a view-only GCMouse read right before the view is built (JayRewd's late mouse idea, §4b).
    - **Gamepad, clipboard, dialogs, the rest:**
      - **Gamepads and joysticks: dropped** (the user, 2026-10-18, §3.1 #12). The next two items are the design as it was:
      - `GCController` (connect/disconnect notifications, `in_joystick`, `in_joystickNo`, `in_availableJoysticks`): the extended gamepad is read in `IN_Frame` as SDL polls its controller. Buttons → `K_PAD0_*` (A/B/X/Y; options = back, home = guide, menu = start; the thumbstick buttons, shoulders, d-pad; paddles, touchpad and the Xbox share button where the profile has them). Sticks and triggers → the same keys and `SE_JOYSTICK_AXIS` code, values scaled to ±32767, y flipped to SDL's down-positive.
      - **Plain HID joysticks** (not gamepads: `K_JOY*`, hats, balls) have no GameController equivalent. Proposed: drop them (`in_joystick` defaults to 0); an IOHIDManager path only if the user wants one (asked at 6f; settled by §3.1 #12: none).
      - Clipboard: `NSPasteboard` (`stringForType:NSPasteboardTypeString`; `clearContents` + `setString:forType:`), the same 4096-byte limit.
      - Dialogs: `Sys_Dialog` stays; it releases the cursor capture first, and leaves exclusive fullscreen if a window exists.
      - `Sys_LoadLibrary` = `dlopen(f, RTLD_NOW | RTLD_LOCAL)` (SDL's flags), `Sys_LoadFunction` = `dlsym` (no underscore retry: `dlsym` takes C names), `Sys_LibraryError` = `dlerror()`; the `DEDICATED` branch unchanged.
      - `sys_main.c`: no SDL version check, no `SDL_Quit`; `Sys_GetProcessorFeatures` returns 0 (what SDL's six queries give on arm64); `Sys_In_Restart_f` checks that a window exists.
      - ~~`openmohaa://`: the Apple-event handler under `#ifdef PROTOCOL_HANDLER`, so turning it on is one CMake line (`PROTOCOL_HANDLER="openmohaa"`). It stays off, as upstream has it, unless the user asks (asked at 6f).~~ Removed with the scheme instead (the user, §3.1 #13).
      - Not in Phase 6: `NSActivityLatencyCritical`, the main thread's QoS and Game Mode on/off (§4b rule 5) are Phase 5 measurements with the user.
    - **What stays:** `glconfig_t` (unchanged); refimport/refexport (`IN_Init` still takes a `void *`, now the `NSWindow`); every cvar and its meaning (`r_mode`, `r_fullscreen`, `r_noborder`, `r_allowResize`, `r_centerWindow`, `r_displayIndex`, `r_metalDrawables`, `r_swapInterval`, `in_mouse`, `in_nograb`, `in_keyboardDebug`, `cl_consoleKeys`; `in_joystick*` and `joy_threshold` only until 6g, with SDL: §3.1 #12); `r_sdlDriver` (read by nothing) goes. The frame order, pacing and usercmd timing don't change.
  - **How it's built and compared:** a CMake option `USE_SDL` (ON until the last slice) picks SDL's files or `code/macos/`'s, and `port/build.sh native` builds the native flavor from the same tree. The `metal` flavor stays SDL, unchanged, until the switch, so the bench, the fixed views and the probe compare both in one sitting (the user's OK above). `port/latency.sh` gets an `OMM_FLAVOR` switch (it always runs `metal` now). The last slice removes SDL and the option; `metal` is then the native build.

Checked without input: the timedemo bench, `port/shot.sh regress` on the native flavor (identical images), each display mode starting and quitting, direct to display in both fullscreen modes (Instruments trace), the modes list against SDL's.

    - **6e, the rest:** `Sys_Dialog` leaving exclusive fullscreen and the capture, ~~the URL handler (off)~~ (`openmohaa://` removed, §3.1 #13), whatever is left; the native flavor is then complete. (Gamepads were planned here; dropped, §3.1 #12.)
    - **6f, with the user:** the latency sitting, SDL (`metal`) against native: the synthetic mouse with `in_mouseRaw 0` in the three display modes at `com_maxfps 250`, then `in_mouseRaw 1` with the user's own mouse; the user's feel test; ~~the `in_mouseRaw` default, `openmohaa://`~~ (decided before 6f: §3.1 #13, #14).
    - **6g, SDL out:** `USE_SDL`, `code/sdl/` (with SDL's joystick and game controller code, §3.1 #12), the vendored SDL headers and libraries, `sdl.cmake`, glcheck's exception, the probe's NSEvent monitor; `otool -L` without SDL; bench, `regress base-s9`, skinab; push. 6g waits for 6f. If the user wants SDL gone before they can do 6f, a `metalref` build of the last SDL commit keeps the baseline measurable (with their OK). *Done in session 24 before the user's half of 6f, their OK (§3.1 #17), with the last SDL commit kept as its own flavor, `sdlref`.*
- **Session 19 result: 6b, the application and the window** (2026-10-18, the user away most of the session; commits e82a1d08, f87f8820 and the docs).
  - **Built:** `USE_SDL` (CMake option in `cmake/platforms/macos.cmake`, default ON; `sdl.cmake` returns early without it and defines `USE_SDL` for the client otherwise); `port/build.sh native` (`-DUSE_SDL=OFF`, its own build and install trees). `client.cmake` and `renderer_metal.cmake` pick the files through `CLIENT_INPUT_SOURCES` and `RENDERER_METAL_WINDOW_SOURCE`, in the same place in the source lists, so the SDL build's object order is unchanged. The shared sys files keep SDL's code in `#ifdef USE_SDL` branches: **`sys_main.c` and `sys_main_new.c` (with `sys_local.h` and `sys_loadlib.h`) preprocess identically before and after for the SDL client and for `omohaaded`** (`clang -E -P` with the build's own flags, both trees laid out alike). So the `metal` flavor is the same SDL code, the baseline for 6f.
  - **`code/macos/` (≈1.1k lines, ARC):**
    - `macos_local.h`: the interface between the three files.
    - `macos_app.m`: `NSApplication` without `run` (regular policy, SDL's three user defaults, the application menu: Hide ⌘H, Hide Others ⌥⌘H, Show All, Quit ⌘Q), a delegate whose `applicationShouldTerminate:` records the quit and returns `NSTerminateCancel`, `finishLaunching`, `activateIgnoringOtherApps:YES` (SDL's call; only "to be deprecated", no warning), an `NSActivityIdleDisplaySleepDisabled` activity (SDL suspends the screen saver with the same kind of power assertion), the pump (`nextEventMatchingMask:… untilDate:nil … dequeue:YES` + `sendEvent:` until empty, one `@autoreleasepool`), the `NSPasteboard` clipboard. Not copied from SDL: its first-pump activation hack (activate the Dock, **`SDL_Delay(300)`**, then the app, read from its `applicationDidFinishLaunching:`), which fixes a menu bar that ignores clicks after a terminal launch. The native app was focused at once in every run (`com_unfocused 0`); whether the menu bar takes clicks after a terminal launch is for the user's look in 6f.
    - `macos_window.m`: the `MTLimp_*` functions, log lines and cvars of `sdl_mtlimp.m` (`r_sdlDriver` gone). An `NSWindow` subclass that can become key when borderless, a layer-hosting content view (`view.layer = CAMetalLayer` before `wantsLayer`; it swallows key events so AppKit doesn't beep), a window delegate (the close button → `[NSApp terminate:]`, i.e. the same quit as ⌘Q; exclusive fullscreen's focus handling; the layer's `contentsScale` on a backing change) and an application-activation observer that deminiaturizes the window (SDL's `focusSomeWindow`). Displays in SDL's order for `r_displayIndex` (the main one, then the others, no mirrors). A window is centered on its display (SDL2 centers `SDL_WINDOWPOS_UNDEFINED` too, so `r_centerWindow` changes nothing in either), or keeps its old top-left across `vid_restart`. Exclusive fullscreen: a borderless window at `CGShieldingWindowLevel()` over the display, no shadow, the dock and menu bar hidden while it is up; the display mode changes only when r_mode's mode isn't the current one (chosen as `SDL_GetClosestDisplayMode` chooses: the smallest mode at least that large in the desktop's format, the requested or desktop refresh, HiDPI before 1×), captured and faded as SDL does it (all displays when it is the main one); on focus loss the desktop mode comes back and the window is minimized, as SDL does. Borderless fullscreen: a titled, resizable window at the desktop size with `FullScreenPrimary`, then `toggleFullScreen:` (a Space). `r_borderlessWindow 1` (archived, latched): borderless fullscreen as a borderless window over the screen instead of a Space (the experiment, below). Windows are created relative to their screen (`initWithContentRect:…screen:`), as SDL creates them; the Space asks for the dock and menu bar hidden (`window:willUseFullScreenPresentationOptions:`), as SDL does. Retina: the drawable is the content size × `backingScaleFactor` (the mode's pixel scale after a mode switch).
    - `macos_input.m`: the `IN_*` functions as a stub layer: the pump in `IN_Frame` (where SDL's was, after `CL_LatencyPump`), the window's events through notifications observed on the main thread inside the pump (focus → `com_unfocused`, miniaturize → `com_minimized`, a resize or backing change → `r_customwidth/height` in points and `vid_restart` after 1 s, comparing **pixels with pixels**: finding 7's resize bug is fixed), the quit request → `quit Closed window` after the pump (EXEC_NOW, as SDL). The input cvars are registered as SDL registers them. No keys, mouse, cursor or gamepads yet (the cursor image is freed at once, not leaked).
    - sys: `dlopen(f, RTLD_NOW | RTLD_LOCAL)`/`dlsym`/`dlerror`; no SDL version check, `SDL_Quit` or CPU feature queries (`Sys_GetProcessorFeatures` returns 0, as SDL's six queries do on arm64); `in_restart` checks `MacOS_WindowExists()`.

The same within the noise (the first native run is the usual first-run high; p99 needs session 17's 8+ alternating runs to compare). Measured on the e82a1d08 build; f87f8820 only changes where windows are created and the Space's presentation options. Worth noting: every SDL run had a 5.0–5.9 ms max frame, every native run 1.2–1.3 ms.

The first native build opened the display-1 window on the main display: `initWithContentRect:…` without `screen:` takes the rect relative to the main screen; created relative to its screen as SDL does (f87f8820).

This Mac has `AppleMenuBarVisibleInFullscreen` 1 (the menu bar stays in fullscreen apps); SDL's Space covered it (0,0 1920×1080, direct, the same sitting) because SDL's window delegate answers `window:willUseFullScreenPresentationOptions:` with full screen + hide dock + hide menu bar. The native delegate does the same now (f87f8820): direct on every frame.

It skips the Space's animation when switching away and at `vid_restart`, and other applications' windows show over it when it isn't in front. **Both go direct, so the choice is the user's** (asked at 6f; *decided in session 21: the Space, §3.1 #15*): the Space stays the default (SDL's behavior, what the user played 3d in), and `r_borderlessWindow 1` (archived, latched) picks the window.

Also for 6f: the menu bar's clicks after a terminal launch (SDL's 300 ms activation hack is left out), `r_borderlessWindow`'s default.
    - Not related to 6b, seen once: `Metal: pipeline cache not written: Failed to generate machO … expecting 'fragment' stage in pipeline no. '14'` at a `vid_restart` save (the first native run of the menu, with a cache from session 18's runs plus 2 new pipelines); the save at quit then wrote a 29 KB archive, and the next run found 1 pipeline in it instead of 13. Not reproduced from a fresh cache or from a full one, with either flavor (the backend code is the same in both). The archive's only pipeline without a fragment function is the depth query's; for the Phase 5 list. *Explained in session 27: an archive read from a file fails to serialize after additions (with or without the query pipeline), and afterwards writes only what was added after the failure; the archive is now written whole (Phase 5 "Session 27 result").*
- **Session 20 result: 6c, the keyboard and text** (2026-10-18, the user at the machine at the start, then away; commits 89f83710 and c7897f30). The design held; one addition (the key releases at the input shutdown, below).
  - **What SDL 2.32 really does** (read from its arm64 binary, `-O0` code with its static symbols: `Cocoa_HandleKeyEvent`, `HandleModifiers`, `IsModifierKeyPressed`, `UpdateKeymap`, `SDL_SendKeyboardKeyInternal`, `SDL_SendKeyboardText`, `SDL_ResetKeyboard`, `-[Cocoa_WindowListener flagsChanged:]`, `-[SDLTranslatorResponder insertText:replacementRange:]`; its `darwin_scancode_table` and `SDL_default_keymap` dumped from the data):
    - **The repeat flag is SDL's own key state**, not `isARepeat`: a key-down of a key that is already down is a repeat, a key-up of a key that isn't down sends nothing, scancode 0 (an unknown key) sends nothing.
    - **Every `flagsChanged` sets all eight modifier sides** from the device-dependent bits, in the order LSHIFT, LCTRL, LALT, LGUI, RSHIFT, RCTRL, RALT, RGUI: a side that is still down sends a repeat key-down (dropped by `sdl_input.c` without a key catcher, a `K_SHIFT`… down again with one). `IsModifierKeyPressed` trusts the device-independent bit when the device bits disagree with it.
    - **Caps lock** is handled in the window listener's `flagsChanged:` (after the eight sides): when `NSEventModifierFlagCapsLock` differs from SDL's `KMOD_CAPS`, a press and a release of `SDL_SCANCODE_CAPSLOCK`; `windowDidBecomeKey:` syncs `KMOD_CAPS` without an event.
    - The layout: `UpdateKeymap` at every key-down that isn't `isARepeat` compares `TISCopyCurrentKeyboardLayoutInputSource()` with the last one by pointer (measured: ~10 ns, the same pointer every call) and rebuilds from the default keymap: `UCKeyTranslate` (key down, no modifiers, no dead keys) for every key whose default key code is a character, keeping it unless the result is empty or 0x10; the ISO swap of scancodes 100 and 53. `KBGetLayoutType(LMGetKbdType()) == kKeyboardISO` also swaps `kVK_ISO_Section`/`kVK_ANSI_Grave` before the table lookup. `SDL_SetKeymap`'s AZERTY digits only change the number row's key codes, which `IN_TranslateSDLToQ3Key` never reads (the number row goes by scancode).
    - Text: `interpretKeyEvents:` on its 0×0 field-editor view (`SDLTranslatorResponder`, an `NSTextInputClient` added as a subview and made first responder) right after the key-down; `insertText:` → `SDL_SendKeyboardText`, which drops text whose first byte is below a space or 127; `doCommandBySelector:` empty; marked text → `SDL_TEXTEDITING` (ignored by the game).
    - Focus loss: `SDL_SetKeyboardFocus(NULL)` → `SDL_ResetKeyboard` (a key-up for every key that is down, by scancode) before `FOCUS_LOST`. At `vid_restart` the same key-ups are queued when its window is destroyed, but `SDL_QuitSubSystem(VIDEO)` then quits its event subsystem (the joystick's reference went first, in `IN_Shutdown`) and the queue is flushed: keys held through a `vid_restart` stay down in the client (no `Key_ClearStates` there).
  - **Built** (`code/macos/`, ≈ +600 lines):
    - `macos_app.m`: the pump hands `keyDown`/`keyUp`/`flagsChanged` to `MacOS_KeyEvent` before `sendEvent:` (which still runs: ⌘ key equivalents reach the menu).
    - `macos_input.m`: SDL's table (128 entries, virtual key code → scancode, copied from its binary), the default key codes, `IN_UpdateKeymap` (SDL's `UpdateKeymap`), `IN_TranslateKey` (`IN_TranslateSDLToQ3Key` case for case, on SDL's numbering), `IN_IsConsoleKey` (verbatim), `IN_SendKey` (SDL's key state + `sdl_input.c`'s `SDL_KEYDOWN`/`SDL_KEYUP`: repeats dropped without a key catcher, `SE_CHAR` ^H / enter / tab / escape / Ctrl+letter, `lastKeyDown`), `IN_SendModifiers` (the eight sides, then caps lock), `IN_ResetKeyboard`, `MacOS_InsertText` (`sdl_input.c`'s `SDL_TEXTINPUT`: UTF-8 → UTF-32, a console character → `K_CONSOLE` press and release, the text after a console key dropped), `in_eventTime` (set after the pump, as SDL), `in_keyboardDebug` (SDL's line without its key names, plus the virtual key code). Focus gain syncs caps lock; focus loss releases every key and drops marked text. **The input shutdown (`vid_restart`, quit) releases every key that is down too**: the key-ups SDL meant to send and lost (above), so no key stays down in the client after a `vid_restart`. `in_restart` keeps the keyboard's state, as SDL's did.
    - `macos_window.m`: the content view is the `NSTextInputClient` (SDL's responder, without the subview: a layer-hosting view's only layer stays the `CAMetalLayer`, which fullscreen needs to go direct to display); the IME candidate window opens at the game's top left, as SDL's did.
    - `cmake/client.cmake`: `-framework Carbon` for the native client (HIToolbox: TIS, `UCKeyTranslate`, `LMGetKbdType`). The native app now links Carbon, as SDL did; still no `libSDL2`.
  - **The check: `port/kbreplay.sh`** (new, committed with `kbreplay.m` and `kbreplay_patch.py`, like `skinab.sh`): two scratch flavors (`kbsdl` = SDL as in `metal`, `kbnat` = native) from the work tree plus a patch that adds the `kbreplay` command; the shipped flavors never have it. The command posts a fixed 63-step sequence of key events into the game's own queue (`[NSApp postEvent:atStart:]`, nothing system-wide), one step per 3 frames right before the input layer pumps, with what a real keyboard's events carry (device-dependent modifier bits, the layout's characters from `UCKeyTranslate` with dead keys on key-down, `isARepeat`), and logs every `SE_KEY`/`SE_CHAR` from `Com_QueueEvent`. It runs in dm/mohdm6 (`g_gametype 0`, `finishloadingscreen`, `ui_console 1`, `in_mouse 0`: no capture) in a 1280×720 window, ~15 s per flavor; the log waits for the game (active, no key catcher) before the first step. **Result: the two streams are identical** up to the input shutdown (214 events: letters, a held key and its repeats dropped in the game, the number row, arrows, the keypad, both sides of Shift/Control/Option/Command including a repeat of a held side, caps lock on and off, Ctrl+A in the game, a focus loss with W and Shift down (both released) and back, the console key opening the console, text, Shift and caps-lock capitals, a held key's repeats kept in the console, backspace, tab, Ctrl+E, the ⌥E dead key then E → `é` (0xE9), digits and punctuation, F1/F5/F12/F13 (→ `PRINT`)/F16 (nothing), the editing keys, enter, `~` closing the console, escape into and out of the menu, and **⌘Q reaching the menu and quitting** (`quit Closed window`) on both). After the input shutdown only the native layer queues the release of ⌘ and Q (the addition above). `in_keyboardDebug` prints the same scancode, key code and Q key on both. Not covered: an ISO keyboard (this Mac's is ANSI; the swap is SDL's code, read from its binary) and a real input method's composition (only the dead key).
  - **Both flavors build** (no new warnings in `code/macos/`); `port/glcheck.sh native` and `metal` clean; the native app's seal verifies.
  - **Bench, same sitting** (unlocked, load 4.5–6.1, the user away): **metal (SDL) 0.220 / 0.221 / 0.220 ms work per frame**, pooled p50/p95/p99/p99.9/max 209/367/484/735/7041 µs; **native 0.217 / 0.215 / 0.217**, pooled 208/363/485/665/1313 µs. Equal; every SDL run again had a 6–7 ms max frame, every native run 1.2–1.3 ms (as in session 19).
  - **`OMM_FLAVOR=native port/shot.sh regress base-s9`: identical to the baseline** except the views that always vary (dm6 view 8: 1998 pixels; dm5's snow: 12–36; dm6fx views 4–7; m2l1 view 0: 11 pixels); dm4 and obj3 identical in every capture.

- **Session 21 result: 6d, the mouse** (2026-10-18, the user at the machine for the GCMouse test and the go-ahead for the runs that take the cursor, then away; commits fc057d1c and b5eb4c9e). The design held, with the changes listed below.
  - **What SDL 2.32 really does** (read from its arm64 binary: `Cocoa_HandleMouseEvent`, `-[Cocoa_WindowListener mouseDown:]`, `mouseUp:`, `mouseMoved:`, `scrollWheel:`, `windowDidBecomeKey:`/`ResignKey:`, `Cocoa_HandleMouseWheel`, `Cocoa_SetRelativeMouseMode`, `Cocoa_WarpMouse(Global)`, `Cocoa_HandleMouseWarp`, `SDL_SetRelativeMouseMode`, `SDL_PerformWarpMouseInWindow`, `SDL_SendMouseMotion`/`SDL_PrivateSendMouseMotion`, `SDL_PrivateSendMouseButton`, `SDL_SendMouseWheel`, `GetScaledMouseDeltas`, `Cocoa_CreateImage`, `-[SDLView resetCursorRects]`):
    - **The captured movement is read in the pump** (`Cocoa_HandleMouseEvent`, before `sendEvent:`): moved and dragged events; **the first one after relative mode is turned on is dropped** (`justEnabledRelative`, set with the warp to the window's center, consumed by the next movement event wherever it comes, even inside `IN_GobbleMotionEvents`); events over the title bar are dropped; **the deltas go through a `float` and then toward zero** (`fcvt s0, d0`, `fcvtzs`: `(int)(float)deltaX`); a warp's offset is added to the next event (`seenWarp`), which never reaches the game (the only warp in relative mode is the capture's, and its event is the dropped one). `SDL_PrivateSendMouseMotion` drops a 0,0 event; the speed and system scales are off without hints, so the deltas reach `sdl_input.c` unchanged.
    - **Buttons, the wheel and the cursor's movement over the window come through the responder chain** (the window listener is the content view's next responder): AppKit's button 0 left, 1 right, 2 middle, n + 1 above (a `Uint8`); a press over the title bar is ignored (a release isn't); SDL's button state drops a press of a button that is down and a release of one that is up. The wheel: `y = deltaY` (the system's direction, natural scrolling included: SDL only reports `isDirectionInvertedFromDevice`), without precise deltas rounded away from zero (a notch is at least one step), added up in a float with the remainder kept and reset on a change of direction; `sdl_input.c` sends one press and release per event with steps. The cursor's position (`mouse->x/y`, `IN_GetMousePosition`) is window points cut toward zero, kept inside the window unless a button is down (SDL's automatic mouse capture); leaving the window, the last position sits at the edge.
    - **Capture** (`Cocoa_SetRelativeMouseMode`): with the keyboard focus, a warp to the window's center (`window->x + w/2`, global points), then `CGAssociateMouseAndMouseCursorPosition(NO)`, whose call right after a warp also ends the warp's event suppression (SDL never sets the suppression interval), then `[NSCursor hide]`. The release: associate, unhide; `SDL_SetRelativeMouseMode` warps back to SDL's own relative position and `sdl_input.c` then warps to `vidWidth/2, vidHeight/2` as points (finding 7: outside the window on a 2× display). A focus loss undoes the system part at once (`windowDidResignKey:`). SDL's grab is a mouse confinement rect (`setMouseConfinementRect:`), moot with the cursor detached.
    - **The cursor:** cursor rects on the content view: the UI's color cursor (an `NSCursor` from the RGBA pixels, alpha premultiplied, the image's size in points) or, while relative, a transparent 16×16 one (`+[NSCursor invisibleCursor]` is SDL's own category, not AppKit's).
  - **Built** (`code/macos/`, ≈ +900 lines, half of them comments):
    - `macos_app.m`: the pump hands moved and dragged events to `MacOS_MouseMotion` before `sendEvent:`.
    - `macos_window.m`: the content view's `mouseDown:` … `otherMouseDragged:`, `scrollWheel:` (to the input layer) and `resetCursorRects` (`MacOS_Cursor()`).
    - `macos_input.m`: `IN_Frame`'s capture rules verbatim (console or UI up in a window, loading in a window, no focus → released), `IN_ActivateMouse`/`IN_DeactivateMouse` with `in_mouse` and `in_nograb` (windowed only), `IN_GobbleMotionEvents` (a pump that drops the movement, and GCMouse's), `IN_Capture` (SDL's relative mode), the release warp to the window's real center; `MacOS_MouseMotion` (SDL's rules above), `IN_MouseAt` (the UI's cursor, **in the drawable's pixels**; `in_nograb`'s deltas in points), `MacOS_MouseButton`/`IN_SendMouseButton` (SDL's numbering and button state; `K_MOUSE1`–5, `K_AUX`), `MacOS_MouseWheel`; the color cursor (`IN_SetCursorFromImage` frees the pixels after copying them: the upstream leak); `IN_IsCursorActive` = captured; a focus loss releases at once; the input shutdown releases the buttons that are down, like the keys (SDL lost those releases). **`in_mouseRaw`** (new, archived, **default 0**): 1 = GCMouse (below). The latency probe's `Sys_MouseEventMonitor`/`Sys_TakeMouseEventTimes` for the native flavor: the times of the movement that reaches the game (`NSEvent.timestamp`, or the GCMouse handler's own `CLOCK_UPTIME_RAW` time).
    - **GCMouse** (`in_mouseRaw 1`, only while captured): `GCMouse.mice` and the connect/disconnect notifications (main queue, so inside the pump), discovery started at `IN_Init` when `in_mouseRaw` is 1; every mouse's handlers on one serial queue at `QOS_CLASS_USER_INTERACTIVE`: the movement added up in floats under an `os_unfair_lock`, the buttons listed in order (left, right, middle, then the auxiliary ones as SDL's 4, 5, …); `IN_Frame` takes them once per pump: the buttons, then one `SE_MOUSE` of the whole counts (the fractions stay), **y negated** (GameController's y points up). While its buttons are used, the button events' presses are ignored (a release still passes through SDL's button state, so a click from a device GCMouse doesn't see can't stick). Without a GCMouse found, the movement events are used. A fullscreen UI (captured, `in_guimouse`) moves its cursor by the movement events, not GCMouse.
    - `cmake/client.cmake`: `-framework GameController` for the native client (SDL linked it; nothing new in the process).
    - `sys_osx.m`: its NSEvent monitor only for SDL and the server (`#if defined(USE_SDL) || defined(DEDICATED)`); **the SDL client's and `omohaaded`'s `sys_osx.m` preprocess identically before and after** (both trees laid out alike).
  - **Changes to the 6a design** (and why):
    - **The UI's cursor position comes from the movement events over the window** (SDL's way), not from `mouseLocationOutsideOfEventStream` at each read: it is the same frame's position, it agrees with the clicks' positions, and posted events can test it; `mouseLocationOutsideOfEventStream` is read once when the window gets the keyboard back (SDL's `windowDidBecomeKey:`).
    - **Buttons and the wheel through the responder chain**, as SDL, not the pump: AppKit's own rules (the title bar, the click that activates the window) stay the same.
    - **The warp's suppression ended SDL's way** (the associate call right after the warp), not with `CGEventSourceSetLocalEventsSuppressionInterval`; **no confinement rect** (the detached cursor can't leave the window).
    - **GCMouse's buttons are used while captured with `in_mouseRaw 1`** (the design's ≥ 0.2 ms rule; measured below); with `in_mouseRaw 0` the buttons stay the button events, like SDL.
    - **The probe's GCMouse time is the handler's own clock read**: `lastEventTimestamp` is wall-clock time (below).
    - Two small fixes over SDL: `in_restart` releases the mouse before reading `in_mouse` again (SDL kept a capture after `in_mouse 0` + `in_restart`); the input shutdown releases the buttons that are down.
    - One startup difference, cosmetic: with `+devmap` in a window the native layer captures for the few frames before the load starts (SDL's window gets the keyboard ~300 ms later, its activation delay), so the cursor is warped to the window's center when the load starts instead of when the map is up.
  - **GCMouse against the button and movement events, with the user's mouse** (`port/gcmtest.m`: a small window that captures the cursor like the game, ~35 s of prompts; the user's mouse is a Model O3 Wired; their HyperX Alloy Origins Core keyboard also shows up as a GCMouse):
    - **y is flipped:** the movement events' y sums −3508 / +1169 / +178 where GCMouse's are +3236 / −1174 / −185 (x: −5374 vs −5371, −187 vs −186).
    - **On this mouse the movement events' deltas are whole numbers and equal GCMouse's counts** (no event below one point, sums within 0.1%): no pointer acceleration shows, so `in_mouseRaw 1` would change the event rate and the latency, not the sensitivity (for the 6f decision). AppKit coalesces the movement: ~190 events/s against GCMouse's ~800/s.
    - **`lastEventTimestamp` is wall-clock time** (Unix seconds), ~30 µs before the handler runs: not `CLOCK_UPTIME_RAW`.
    - **Clicks** (6 left, 6 right): GCMouse's handler ran 0.3–3.8 ms after the button event's own timestamp; the button event reached the (idle) main thread 0.6–4.1 ms after it; **GCMouse was earlier by 0.05–3.6 ms, ~1.8 ms on average**. The movement's arrival times weren't measured (6f, with the probe).
  - **The check: `port/kbreplay.sh mouse win|full|hidpi`** (new; `msreplay` in `port/kbreplay.m`): 40 steps posted into the game's own queue, SDL (`kbsdl`) against native (`kbnat`), `in_mouse 1`, `in_mouseRaw 0`. Building the events took some finding (gotchas below): a CGEvent alone gets no window (AppKit drops its clicks and scrolls), its window field gives a wrong point, and its delta fields are integers; the replay puts the CGEvent's fields on a mouse event AppKit made for the game window, so the deltas are whole. The steps: the first movement after the capture (dropped), whole and small moves, a 0,0 move, fast moves, buttons 1–5, 6 and 8, a button pressed twice, drags, moves between button events, wheel notches, fine scrolling adding up and changing direction, Escape into the menu (released, warped to the center), the menu cursor over the window, at the bottom right corner, leaving on the right and coming back, leaving at the top left, clicks, a drag out of the window and back, the wheel, Escape back (the first movement dropped again), the console (released), Cmd+Q with the left button held.
    - **Window (1280×720 on the 1× display) and exclusive fullscreen (1920×1080): the streams are identical** (78 and 79 events). In fullscreen the menu stays captured and its cursor moves by the deltas (clicks skipped there: they could hit anything).
    - **The 2× display (`r_displayIndex 1`): every `SE_` event is identical; only the UI's cursor differs, as designed**: SDL gives points in a 2560×1440 UI (101,50 for a point at 101.7,50.2; the top left quarter only), native gives pixels (203,100); after the release SDL's warp to (1280,720) points left the window and its cursor stayed at 368,39, native warps to the real center (1280,720 pixels).
    - After the input shutdown only native releases the held Q, Command and left button (shown, not compared). The keyboard replay (`port/kbreplay.sh run`) is still identical (63 steps, 214 events).
    - **Not replayed:** fractional deltas (posted events can't carry them; the cut is SDL's expression, read from its binary), GCMouse (the HID devices below the WindowServer; the user's mouse, 6f), `in_nograb`.
  - **Both flavors build** (no warnings in `code/macos/`); `port/glcheck.sh native` and `metal` clean; the native app links GameController and Carbon, still no `libSDL2`, its seal verifies.
  - **Bench, same sitting** (unlocked, load 3.7–5.5, the user away): **metal (SDL) 0.222 / 0.228 / 0.238 ms work per frame**, pooled p50/p95/p99/p99.9/max 217/395/515/789/6996 µs; **native 0.234 / 0.219 / 0.229**, pooled 212/397/574/1339/5226 µs (its first run's p99 809, the usual first-run high; runs 2–3: 502/518). Equal. **The max frame is the timedemo's first frame in every run of both flavors: the mouse capture** (the warp, the detach and the hide are synchronous WindowServer calls): 4.3–5.2 ms native, 4.6–7.0 ms SDL, once per capture. Native had no capture before 6d, hence its 1.2–1.3 ms max in sessions 19–20.
  - **`OMM_FLAVOR=native port/shot.sh regress base-s9`: identical to the baseline** except the views that always vary (dm6 view 8: 1998 pixels; dm5's snow: 26–44; dm6fx views 4–7; m2l1 view 0: 10 pixels); dm4 and obj3 identical in every capture.

- **Session 22 result: 6e, the rest** (2026-10-18, the user at the machine; they said yes to the runs that take the screen, mouse and keyboard and kept off the input meanwhile; commits 27535df9, d951aab3, bec46fd9, then the user's decisions from session 21: f99feea0, 34fabd52, 0740e6b9, db88239d, and the docs). **The native flavor is complete**: everything SDL's files do for the game, except the gamepads and joysticks (§3.1 #12). The design held; `Sys_Dialog` got one addition (the keyboard given back afterwards, below).
  - **`Sys_Dialog` with the game up.** The only callers with a window up are `Sys_Error`'s (`Sys_ErrorDialog`): a fatal `Com_Error` shuts the client and its window down first, so its dialog comes without a window; with one: a recursive error, an error during the start after the window is up. The startup's "Abnormal Exit" question comes before any window. **The check, `port/kbreplay.sh dialog <mode>`** (new; `dlgtest` in `port/kbreplay.m`): once the game has had the captured mouse for 100 frames, an OK/Cancel `Sys_Dialog`; a timer in the modal loop logs what the screen shows (the dialog's window and every on-screen window in front of it that overlaps it, from `CGWindowListCopyWindowInfo`; the cursor, `CGCursorIsVisible`; the main display's mode and capture) and presses Cancel (`performClick:` on the alert's button, a click's path); then the game is logged for 250 frames. Both flavors, `in_mouse 1`, `in_mouseRaw 0`.

    | Mode | SDL (the `metal` flavor, unchanged): the dialog | the cursor during it | afterwards | Native (27535df9) |
    |---|---|---|---|---|
    | window 1280×720 | in front | shown | the game window without the keyboard: unfocused, released, until the user clicks it | in front, cursor shown; afterwards key and captured the next frame |
    | exclusive 1920×1080 (SDL captures the displays even at the desktop size) | in front: taking the keyboard, the alert minimizes the game and releases the displays (SDL's focus loss) | **hidden** | minimized; the cursor still hidden over the desktop | in front over the game, which stays (normal level, not minimized); cursor shown; afterwards exclusive again, key, captured |
    | exclusive 1280×720 (the mode switched) | in front, the desktop mode back (the focus loss) | hidden | minimized, hidden | the desktop mode back for the dialog, cursor shown; afterwards 1280×720 again, key, captured |
    | the Space | in front, in the Space | shown | without the keyboard | in front, cursor shown; afterwards key, captured |

    Only the Dock's full-screen window (layer 20; its transparent overlay) is listed in front of the dialog, in both layers and every mode but the Space (nothing there). **What the native layer does now:** `MacOS_DialogBegin` (called by `Sys_Dialog` before `runModal`, under `!USE_SDL && !DEDICATED`) releases the capture as a focus loss does (the cursor where it was parked, the window's center; without a pump: a dialog can come from anywhere) and leaves exclusive fullscreen without minimizing (`MTLimp_LeaveExclusive( qfalse )`: the presentation options, the desktop's mode and the displays back, the window at the normal level under the alert; `windowDidResignKey` then finds it left already). `MacOS_DialogEnd` (after `runModal`) makes the game window key again, unless it is minimized or the game is in the background: **AppKit gives the keyboard to no window after an alert** (the first runs, without `MacOS_DialogEnd`, showed it for both layers, with the modal loop stopped by code and with the button pressed), and the window getting it brings exclusive fullscreen back (`windowDidBecomeKey`) and the capture (`IN_Frame`).
    - **The startup's "Abnormal Exit" question** (`dialog start`: an empty PID file, which counts as stale; the app binary started directly, since `run.sh` deletes PID files; a timer installed before `main`, `OMM_DLGTEST`, answers No): the same in both layers, unchanged: no application or window yet, the app not active, so the alert opens at the normal level without the keyboard (behind other applications' windows after a terminal launch); the game then starts normally and quits cleanly. Upstream's behavior; activating the application first would make it differ from SDL for no reported problem, so it stays.
    - **The SDL client and `omohaaded` preprocess identically before and after** (`clang -E -P` with each target's flags from the compilation database, HEAD against the work tree, both laid out alike: `build.noindex/s22/pp.py`): `sys_osx.m`, `sys_main.c`, `sys_unix.c`, `con_log.c`, `con_tty.c`, `new/sys_main_new.c`, `new/sys_unix_new.c` for both, plus the client's `sdl_input.c`, `sdl_mtlimp.m` and `cl_main.cpp` (every client and server source that includes `sys_local.h`). So the `metal` flavor is the same SDL code, the 6f baseline.
  - **`openmohaa://`: added off, then removed on the user's decision** (§3.1 #13). First (d951aab3): SDL registers a `GURL`/`GURL` handler on its app delegate when it creates it, before `finishLaunching` (`Cocoa_RegisterApp` in its binary), and turns the link into `SDL_DROPFILE`, which `sdl_input.c` hands to `Sys_ParseProtocolUri`; the native app delegate got the same handler under `PROTOCOL_HANDLER` (undefined, as upstream), checked in a scratch build with the define (`urltest`: link Apple events sent to the game's own process, `connect` replaced by a logger: the good links reached `connect`, the bad ones were refused, nothing connected). Then the user, told about it in session 21, asked to remove it instead: f99feea0 takes the scheme out of the Info.plist (`CFBundleURLTypes`, both flavors; `plutil -lint` passes, the seals verify), deletes `Sys_ParseProtocolUri` and the `PROTOCOL_HANDLER` blocks of `sys_main.c` and `sys_local.h`, reverts the native handler and drops `port/kbreplay.sh url`. The always-on `--uri` guard and `sdl_input.c`'s compiled-out blocks stay until 6g.
  - **The user's other decisions from session 21, applied** (§3.1 #14–#16): `in_mouseRaw` defaults to 1 (34fabd52; `port/latency.sh` now sets `in_mouseRaw 0` whenever it moves the synthetic mouse, which GCMouse never sees; `kbreplay.sh`'s mouse and dialog runs set it already; the bench and the fixed views have no mouse input). `r_borderlessWindow` stays 0 (no change). The dead update checker and curl code are deleted (0740e6b9: 30 files, ~6.8k lines, mostly curl's headers): the sources and headers, `code/curl`, `code/thirdparty/curl-8.15.0`, `cmake/libraries/curl.cmake`, the `USE_HTTP` option and its CMake blocks, the hooks in `sys_main_new.c` (`Sys_InitEx`, `Sys_ShutdownEx` and `Sys_ProcessBackgroundTasks` stay, the first with its localization), `sys_local_new.h`'s declarations, `cl_main.cpp`'s `CL_VerifyUpdate` (empty since session 16) and its call and includes, and ioq3's `USE_CURL` blocks in `client.h` and `cl_main.cpp`. libmad's example program no longer gets a target (db88239d: `EXAMPLE` off before `add_subdirectory`; it was configured, with a policy warning, never built).
  - **The SDL build compiles to the same code before and after all of it** (`build.noindex/s22/ppall.py`: every C, C++ and Objective-C source in the `metal` flavor's compilation database outside `code/thirdparty`, 547 of the client, `omohaaded` and the game modules, preprocessed from a `git archive HEAD` tree and from the same plus the work tree's changes, `__FILE__` mapped to one prefix and blank lines ignored; where that differs, the LLVM IR compared, without the module's name and ThinLTO's summary, whose GUIDs of `static` symbols hash the file's path): 530 preprocess identically; 16 preprocess differently by removals only (14 by `sys_local_new.h`'s three checker declarations, included through `sys_local.h`; `sys_main_new.c`, client and server, also by the curl and checker headers' declarations) and compile to identical IR; `cl_main.cpp` loses the checker's header (its C++ classes and the `<thread>` family), `CL_VerifyUpdate` and its call, and its IR differs only by that empty function's definition (the call was inlined away before). The `metal` flavor's compile commands are the same, minus libmad's example. So the SDL flavor stays the 6f baseline.
  - **The inventory, a last pass:** every function SDL's files export has a native one (`IN_Init`, `IN_Shutdown`, `IN_Restart`, `IN_Frame`, `IN_GetMousePosition`, `IN_SetCursorFromImage`, `IN_FreeCursor`, `IN_IsCursorActive`; `MTLimp_Init`, `MTLimp_Shutdown`, `MTLimp_EndFrame`, `MTLimp_Minimize`, `MTLimp_SetGamma`, `MTLimp_LogComment`; `nm -gU` of the objects) except SDL's own `SDL_window`, `stick_state` (the joystick) and `r_sdlDriver` (read by nothing). Every cvar of "What stays" is registered with SDL's default and flags (`r_allowResize`, `r_centerWindow`, `r_displayIndex`, `r_metalDrawables` latched and archived; `in_mouse`, `in_nograb`, `in_keyboardDebug` archived; `r_availableModes` ROM; `cl_consoleKeys`, `r_mode`, `r_fullscreen`, `r_noborder`, `r_swapInterval` read the same way), the joystick ones aside (`in_joystick`, `in_joystickNo`, `in_joystickUseAnalog`, `joy_threshold`, `in_availableJoysticks`: §3.1 #12). The log lines are SDL's but for its own error strings (`SDL_Init…`, `SDL_CreateWindow failed`, "SDL using driver", the joystick lines) and "Display supports any resolution" (a mode without a size, which macOS never reports); the native layer adds "Couldn't change the display mode", "No display mode for WxH", "Display mode WxH (… pixels) at N Hz" and "Couldn't create the window". Nothing is missing.
  - **The native layer's differences from SDL, on purpose** (all of them, sessions 19–22):
    1. The application: no first-pump 300 ms delay (SDL's activation hack for a menu bar that ignores clicks after a terminal launch: the user's look, 6f); a minimal application menu (Hide, Hide Others, Show All, Quit); the display kept awake by an `NSActivityIdleDisplaySleepDisabled` activity (SDL: a power assertion of the same kind).
    2. Exclusive fullscreen changes the display mode, and captures the displays, only when `r_mode`'s size isn't the current mode's; SDL switched to the same mode too, so it also captured the displays at the desktop size (seen again in the dialog check).
    3. `r_borderlessWindow` (new, archived, latched, default 0 = the Space, SDL's, the user's choice, §3.1 #15): 1 = borderless fullscreen as a borderless window over the screen; both go direct to display.
    4. Retina (finding 7): the resize check compares pixels with pixels and a backing-scale change queues `vid_restart` (SDL compared points with pixels); the UI's cursor is in the drawable's pixels on a 2× display (SDL: points, the top left quarter only) and the release warp goes to the window's real center (SDL's left the window there).
    5. The input shutdown (`vid_restart`, quit) releases the keys and mouse buttons that are down (SDL queued those releases and lost them, so they stayed down in the client after a `vid_restart`).
    6. `in_restart` releases the mouse before reading `in_mouse` again (SDL kept its capture after `in_mouse 0` + `in_restart`).
    7. `in_mouseRaw` (new, archived, **default 1**, the user's choice, §3.1 #14): GCMouse's raw deltas and, while captured, its buttons; 0 = the movement events' deltas cut as SDL cuts them.
